**[00:01:33.770] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000039)  
Erwin M. Garcia. The budget of the Commission on Elections for fiscal year 2027 amounting

**[00:01:39.850] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000040)  
to 29.5 billion pesos, inclusive of the automatic appropriations for retirement and life insurance

**[00:01:45.930] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000041)  
premiums amounting to 339.88 million pesos. In terms of new appropriations, which is subject

**[00:01:53.930] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000042)  
for congressional approval, the total budget of the COMELEC amounts to 29.16 billion pesos

**[00:02:01.130] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000043)  
representing an increase of about 46.7 percent or 9.3 billion pesos higher than the

**[00:02:08.490] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000044)  
fiscal year 2026 level of 19.87 billion pesos 23.92 billion pesos for the preparatory national

**[00:02:18.250] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000045)  
and local elections is included for the preparations for the 2028 elections.

**[00:02:24.730] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000046)  
The COMELEC is the principal government agency tasked to enforce and administer all laws and

**[00:02:30.090] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000047)  
regulations concerning the conduct of regular and special elections. Under our Constitution,

**[00:02:36.090] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000048)  
it is a body designed to be independent from the executive, legislative, and

**[00:02:40.650] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000049)  
judicial branches of government to ensure the conduct of free, fair, and honest elections.

**[00:02:46.090] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000050)  
More importantly, our Constitution grants fiscal autonomy to the COMELEC to enable them to operate

**[00:02:51.770] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000051)  
effectively, efficiently, and free

**[00:02:53.930] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000052)  
from political interference. It provides that funds certified by the Commission as necessary

**[00:03:01.130] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000053)  
to defray the expenses for holding regular and special elections, plebiscites, initiatives, referenda

**[00:03:08.170] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000054)  
and recalls will be provided in the regular or special appropriations

**[00:03:12.570] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000055)  
and once approved, will be released automatically upon certification by the Chairman of the

**[00:03:17.690] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000056)  
Commission. With the presence of our COMELEC officials, we look forward to an efficient and

**[00:03:23.930] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-000057)  
fruitful discussion. Thank you po.

initial statement i would like to respectfully announce to you

**[00:03:55.210] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000062)  
Commission election during the last year of fiscal year 2025. For 2025, of course, we conducted the

**[00:04:01.750] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000063)  
national and local elections, a very successful election. It was actually the fastest in the

**[00:04:07.290] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000064)  
history of our country in a midterm election. We were able to proclaim the senator in five days.

**[00:04:13.810] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000065)  
We were able to proclaim the mayors and the governors on the night of the election or the

**[00:04:18.370] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000066)  
next day after the election. We were able to proclaim the party's congressman seven days

**[00:04:23.830] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000067)  
after the election. Indeed, actually, it likewise resulted to an 81.7 percent. Voters turned out the

**[00:04:31.950] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000068)  
highest in a midterm election. Our highest was the 2022 election, the presidential election, which

**[00:04:38.170] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000069)  
was actually 83 percent. Your Comelec was likewise awarded the commission of the year by an

**[00:04:47.410] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000070)  
international body, and it was likewise recognized by electing us for the first time in the history

**[00:04:54.490] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000074)  
Comelec, and the history likewise of the world electoral body and the association of electoral

**[00:04:58.690] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000075)  
authorities. The Comelec was elected both chairperson in both of these world electoral bodies. So we

**[00:05:07.310] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000076)  
will be assuming chairmanship by the month of October. That's actually a recognition, perhaps,

**[00:05:15.930] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000077)  
of what the Comelec is doing, and perhaps they're likewise using the Comelec's model as their model

**[00:05:21.550] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000078)

**[00:05:23.470] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000079)

**[00:13:30.610] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000179)  
So ngayon po gusto namin sana mas maaga tayo mag-file.

**[00:13:34.010] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000180)  
Para maaga po namin makumpleto ang list of candidates.

**[00:13:37.870] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000181)  
At the same time, makumpleto rin yung list of voters po natin.

**[00:13:40.750] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000182)  
So therefore, critical po ang 2027 sa preparation natin.

**[00:13:46.270] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000183)  
Hindi po dapat pinagkakasya ang lahat sa 2028.

**[00:13:50.390] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000184)  
Kasi po sa 2028, kung ang election natin is May,

**[00:13:53.990] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000185)  
napakahira po na January, February, March, magpuprint ka ng balota.

**[00:13:58.550] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000186)  
Pag may nagka-problema, you have to repeat.

**[00:14:00.530] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000187)  
And magkakakompromise yung printing natin ng balota.

**[00:14:03.690] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000188)  
So dito po sa makina namin, ang projection po namin,

**[00:14:06.270] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000189)  
sa 1.51 billion,

**[00:14:10.910] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000190)  
yung pong dapat na machines natin for 2028 election ay 124,000.

**[00:14:16.270] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000191)  
But because of the cut of from 23 billion to 16, magiging 21.66,

**[00:14:25.070] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000192)  
8,000 po more or less ang mawawalang machines sa Comelec.

**[00:14:29.590] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000193)  
Pagka po hindi po naibalik sa amin yun,

**[00:14:32.070] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000194)  
we will resort to likewise increasing the number of voters per precinct muli.

**[00:14:37.550] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000195)  
Ang plano po sana ni Comelec, 600 voters lang per precinct.

**[00:14:41.590] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000196)  
Para po mas maliging maayos ang pagboto ng mga kababayan natin.

**[00:14:44.870] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000197)  
But if not,

**[00:14:45.990] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000198)  
the budget will not be restored, 800 to 1,000 pumuli.

**[00:14:49.690] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000199)  
So masisisi na naman po si Comelec bakit ganun kadami ang votante per precinct,

**[00:14:54.190] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000200)  
kahit yung mga nakatatanda may kapansanan,

**[00:14:56.390] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000201)  
simply because what was given to us only was

**[00:15:00.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000202)  
21.66 billion, less than 1.51 billion.

**[00:15:04.520] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000203)  
So ang sa amin po, ito po talaga ipinapakiusap namin na sana man lang makapag-list tayo ng 124,310 machines

**[00:15:15.500] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000204)  
sapagkat yung 10,000 po dyan ay contingency machines.

**[00:15:19.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000205)  
We need contingency machines.

**[00:15:21.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000206)  
For the first time also in 2025, your honors, tayo po ay nagkaroon ng 10 machines bawat munisipyo as contingency.

**[00:15:32.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000207)  
Nagkaroon din po tayo ng contingency machines sa lahat ng provincial offices of the commission.

**[00:15:37.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000208)  
Meaning to say, kung nagka-problema sa Cebu halimbawa na anuman po ang nangyari, naandyan lagi yung machine na pwede namin ipampalit.

**[00:15:45.500] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000209)  
Wala po tayong naging problema na major during the last election of 2025.

**[00:15:50.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000210)

**[00:15:56.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000211)

**[00:16:05.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000212)

**[00:16:14.760] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000213)  
Yung po next, your honors, if I may please invite your attention.

**[00:16:19.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000214)

**[00:16:20.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000215)  
E yung pong printing of ballots.

**[00:16:23.360] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000216)  
Ibig sabihin po, ang nire-request po namin is 3.2 billion.

**[00:16:28.020] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000217)

**[00:16:32.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000218)  
1.7 to 1.8.

**[00:16:34.480] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000219)  
At 800 million po ang ating voters information sheet.

**[00:16:38.540] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000220)  
Yan po yung four pages na pinamamahagi natin sa lahat ng botante.

**[00:16:42.220] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000221)

**[00:16:44.500] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000222)  
At pagkatapos po, magpiprint din kami ng pampaskil sa labas ng presinto na list of voters.

**[00:16:48.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000223)  
At yung mismo nasa loob.

**[00:16:50.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000224)

**[00:16:53.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000225)  
That's why we asked for 3.2 billion.

**[00:16:55.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000226)  
Binigyan lang po kami, your honors, ng 348.

**[00:17:01.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000227)  
348.

**[00:17:02.360] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000228)  
Ibig sabihin po, hindi po kami makakapagprint ng balota by 2027.

**[00:17:08.080] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000229)

**[00:17:15.020] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000230)

**[00:17:26.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000232)

**[00:17:29.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000233)

**[00:17:32.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000234)

**[00:17:37.320] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000235)

**[00:17:41.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000236)

**[00:17:43.440] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000237)  
So, critical po na maiprepare na natin ang ballots by December,

**[00:17:47.500] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000238)

**[00:17:50.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000239)

**[00:17:55.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000240)

**[00:18:00.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000241)

**[00:18:05.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000242)

**[00:18:10.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000243)  
So, again, tinanggalan po kami ng more or less 2.93 billion pesos sa printing.

**[00:18:20.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000245)

**[00:20:49.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000272)

31 million.

**[00:24:44.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000333)  
Project of precinct.

**[00:24:46.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000334)  
Hindi na po namin magagawa dahil po zero po kami lahat-lahat yan.

**[00:24:49.440] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000335)  
Your honors, lahat po yan ay 9 billion.

**[00:24:52.500] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000336)  
Yan po ang preparatory national and local election.

**[00:24:55.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000337)

**[00:25:01.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000338)

**[00:25:03.480] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000339)  
9 plus 6, that's 15 billion po ang natanggal sa amin.

**[00:25:09.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000340)  
Wala po kami pwedeng maipagawa.

**[00:25:10.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000341)  
Zero po kami.

**[00:25:12.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000342)  
For your information,

**[00:25:13.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000343)  
yung honors,

**[00:25:14.020] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000344)

**[00:25:19.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000345)

**[00:25:21.840] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000346)  
nihingi po ng tulong sa inyo para malagyan sila ng budget.

**[00:25:25.100] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000347)  
Zero po yung kami.

**[00:25:25.860] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000348)  
Wala po kami field offices na pwedeng ipagawa for next year.

**[00:25:29.440] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000349)  
In fact, yung pong pinapagawa namin Comelec building,

**[00:25:32.220] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000350)

**[00:25:35.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000351)

**[00:25:37.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000352)  
your honors, sa kasalukuyan.

**[00:25:39.460] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000353)  
Kung tatanungin niyo po kami,

**[00:25:40.460] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000354)

**[00:25:43.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000355)

**[00:25:44.420] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000356)  
Okay na po yun.

**[00:25:45.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000357)

**[00:25:47.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000358)  
Pero huwag naman po sana,

**[00:25:49.920] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000359)  
yung PNLE,

**[00:25:52.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000360)  
Preparatory National and Local Election.

**[00:25:54.760] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000361)

**[00:25:56.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000362)  
Sa presidential and vice-presidential election.

**[00:25:59.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000363)

**[00:26:02.540] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000364)  
And therefore,

Because,

**[00:26:30.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000378)  
definitely,

the conduct of the election

the,

,

**[00:26:46.740] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000387)

**[00:26:49.420] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-000389)

**[00:58:12.850] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001103)

**[00:58:36.410] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001115)

**[00:58:38.550] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001116)

ay 16% lang

**[00:58:50.530] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001122)  
ng ating national

**[00:58:52.370] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001123)

**[00:59:02.250] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001128)

**[00:59:04.010] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001129)  
64%. Ang

**[00:59:06.230] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001130)

**[00:59:22.010] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001138)

**[00:59:26.570] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001140)  
99.97%

**[00:59:28.990] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001141)  
yung accuracy.

**[00:59:30.630] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001142)  
At kung

**[00:59:31.790] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001143)

**[00:59:34.030] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001144)

**[00:59:36.010] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001145)

**[00:59:37.810] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001146)  
So, bakit kailangan

**[00:59:40.010] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001147)

**[00:59:41.930] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001148)  
samantalang if we will be

**[00:59:43.870] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001149)  
spending less than

**[00:59:46.190] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001150)  
17.8

**[00:59:48.250] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001151)  
billion, magkakaroon kayo na savings

**[00:59:50.230] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001152)  
na mga 6 billion na magagamit

**[00:59:52.350] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001153)  
nyo doon sa mga pangangailangan nyo na sinasabi nyo.

**[00:59:54.930] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001154)  
Salamat, Mr. General.

**[00:59:56.270] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001155)  
Talaga pong natutuwa kami

**[00:59:58.430] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001156)  
doon sa inyo pong statement

**[01:00:00.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001157)  
In fact, yan din po sana yung pinopropose namin nung nag-defend kami ng budget

**[01:00:04.100] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001158)  
nung nakaraang budget season for the 2025 election.

**[01:00:08.940] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001159)  
Kung kaya nga lang po, siyempre the Congress has the final say as regards the budget.

**[01:00:14.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001160)  
So, una, hindi po kami pinayagan noon na bumili.

**[01:00:17.480] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001161)  
Tama naman po, lease lang.

**[01:00:18.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001162)  
Number two, one-time lease only.

**[01:00:21.080] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001163)  
Kasi nga po, walang option to purchase, lease only.

**[01:00:23.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001164)  
So, this is not saying po na ayaw namin doon sa dati nating provider.

**[01:00:32.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001165)  
Kasi nga po, maganda naman ang performance.

**[01:00:34.540] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001166)  
Pero siyempre po, hindi pwede magsabi na kami ngayon na dapat sila pa rin

**[01:00:37.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001167)  
dahil basta sabihin, tailor-feated yung aming terms of reference.

**[01:00:42.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001168)  
Kung kaya lang po, siyempre Mr. Chair, yung lahat po ng desisyon patungkol sa budget,

**[01:00:48.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001169)  
although we will propose,

**[01:00:49.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001170)  
ang may final say pa rin po is the power of the first Congress.

**[01:00:53.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001171)  
Pag sinabi po sa amin na only one-time use, yung po talaga one-time use lang.

**[01:00:59.160] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001172)  
At for the information also of the...

**[01:01:01.440] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001173)  
Excuse me po, sinabi ba ng Congress na one-time use lang?

**[01:01:04.260] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001174)  
Wala pong ganyang, wala akong pagkakaalam na sinasabi yan ng Congress.

**[01:01:07.600] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001175)  
Ang alam ko po, kayong gagawa ng terms of reference at yung term of reference,

**[01:01:11.740] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001176)  
pag sinabi niyong pwede ang second hand, pwede sila mag-bid.

**[01:01:14.920] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001177)  
Siyempre po, Mr. Chair, lahat po ng mga gagamitin, lalo sa gobyerno, dapat laging bago.

**[01:01:20.840] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001178)  
And therefore, una, hindi na kami pinayagan.

**[01:01:23.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001179)  
Nga po ni Congress na bumili, hindi...

**[01:01:25.300] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001180)  
Mathematic po.

**[01:01:27.260] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001181)  
Ginamit po uli yun.

**[01:01:28.580] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001182)  
Ginamit po uli yun.

**[01:01:30.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001183)  
Although may purchase agreement, second hand purchase agreement,

**[01:01:34.820] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001184)  
ginamit po uli yun.

**[01:01:37.840] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001185)  
Kailangan po natin ng austerity ngayon.

**[01:01:41.280] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001186)  
Alam nyo, from 17.9, gagasos tayo ng 23 billion,

**[01:01:48.060] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001187)  
nandiyan na yung makina na ayos naman.

**[01:01:50.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001188)  
So, this is the same argument.

**[01:01:53.140] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001189)  
I've been telling you, during the Smartmatic, na merong pwede pang gamitin,

**[01:01:59.340] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001190)  
makakatipid tayo, magagasos lang natin, mga 10 billion, as against 18 billion.

**[01:02:05.700] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001191)  
O ngayon po, ganito na naman.

**[01:02:07.360] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001192)  
We will be spending 23 billion as against the machine na pinaglaban nyo noon.

**[01:02:12.860] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001193)  
Hindi po ba?

**[01:02:13.960] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001194)  
So, ano po ba ang sabi ng Comelec Advisory Council tungkol dito?

**[01:02:18.660] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001195)  
Nakonbinyo na po ba ang Comelec Advisory Council?

**[01:02:21.360] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001196)  
Ay yes po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:02:23.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001197)  
Tapos na po kami sa Terms of Reference ngayon.

**[01:02:26.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001198)  
Nagbigay na rin ng suggestion, recommendation ng Comelec Advisory Council.

**[01:02:30.800] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001199)  
At ang kanila pong recommendation, parehas po.

**[01:02:32.860] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001200)  
We have to lease, not necessarily from the same provider,

**[01:02:37.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001201)  
we have to lease the machines for the 2028 elections.

**[01:02:41.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001202)  
In fact, nagpadagdag po sila ng ibang mga features doon sa mga ililist natin sa machines.

**[01:02:48.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001203)  
So, sila rin mismo ay nag-recommend din po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:02:53.140] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001204)  
Bago dito na we will need additional mga 6 billion.

**[01:02:59.180] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001205)  
Anong bago dito sa makina na ito?

**[01:03:02.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001206)  
Mr. Chair, hindi lang po ito yung technical features.

**[01:03:06.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001207)  
Kasi nga po, mag-i-increase ang number of voters.

**[01:03:10.760] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001208)  
So, dati-dati po, Mr. Chair, mag-i-increase po yung number of voters.

**[01:03:13.800] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001209)  
Kaya po nag-i-increase yung machine natin.

**[01:03:16.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001210)  
Tingnan nyo po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:03:16.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001211)  
Dati-dati ang ating presinto ay 101,000 plus 70.

**[01:03:23.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001212)  
7,000 na contingency machines.

**[01:03:26.480] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001213)  
That's more or less 108,000.

**[01:03:29.420] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001214)  
Ngayon po, Mr. Chair, 124,000.

**[01:03:33.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001215)  
124,000 machines ang kailangan po natin.

**[01:03:38.300] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001216)  
Nung nakaraan, ang ating machine per machine natin ay 155,000.

**[01:03:46.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001217)  
Ngayon po, papagbabagsak lang po siya ng mga 160,000.

**[01:03:50.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001218)  
5,000 lang po na dagdag.

**[01:03:53.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001219)  
Kung i-compute po natin.

**[01:03:55.460] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001220)  
So, it's not really because of the technical improvements.

**[01:03:58.800] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001221)  
Although, malaking bagay yun.

**[01:04:00.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001222)  
Yung technical improvements, hindi po palagay namin makaka-apekto sa pagpe-presyo

**[01:04:06.040] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001223)  
nung magpo-procure sa amin.

**[01:04:08.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001224)  
Kung hindi yung bilang talaga ng dami po, Mr. Chair, ng mga makina.

**[01:04:12.680] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001225)  
If you will need additional 20,000 machines, that's only 3 billion.

**[01:04:18.760] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001226)  
At kung ang ililis nyo, yung lumana,

**[01:04:22.260] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001227)  
at least,

**[01:04:23.140] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001228)  
makakatipid kayo, baka higit po sa 3 billion.

**[01:04:27.460] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001229)  
So, ibig sabihin, makakatipid kayo dito.

**[01:04:31.320] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001230)  
More or less, pinakamababa, 6 billion kung gagamitin nyo yung dating machine.

**[01:04:37.960] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001231)  
So, instead of magkagulong eleksyon, dahil kulang yung machine,

**[01:04:44.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001232)  
kaysa magkaroon ng problema yung eleksyon, dahil kulang sa pondo,

**[01:04:48.900] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001233)  
bakit hindi tayo magtipid, gamitin natin yung pwede pa naman.

**[01:04:51.940] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001234)  
Totally.

**[01:04:53.140] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001235)  
Meron naman kayong technical working group na makikita kung talagang pwede pa yung mga dati.

**[01:04:59.860] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001236)  
Payagan nyo lang na yan ay magamit muli.

**[01:05:04.600] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001237)  
At hindi naman tayo parang si action aksaya.

**[01:05:07.360] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001238)  
Eh, palagay ko, magkakasya kung kulangin man yung pondo ninyo, kaunti lamang.

**[01:05:14.300] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001239)  
Ngayon po, kung magkailangan nyo na napakalaking pondo,

**[01:05:18.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001240)  
i-restore yung 9 billion, tingin ko napakaliit ng fiscal space natin.

**[01:05:22.880] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001241)  
Gaya ng sinabi ko sa inyo, only nasa 16% na lang po yung fiscal space for capital outlay.

**[01:05:32.880] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001242)  
Kaya nga pati yung Comilic building nyo nawala.

**[01:05:35.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001243)  
Salamat po.

**[01:05:36.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001244)  
Papasalamat po kami doon sa concern ng honorable member of this committee.

**[01:05:42.820] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001245)  
And definitely, we will consider that for purposes of finalizing everything.

**[01:05:48.020] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001246)  
Tutal naman po, Mr. Chair, kung sakali po hindi ma-re-restore,

**[01:05:51.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001247)  
so we will have to really find.

**[01:05:52.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001248)  
A reasonable plan of action, kung talaga pong hindi ma-re-restore yung budget natin.

**[01:05:59.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001249)  
Ang sa amin lang po is, at least, nandito pa naman po kami sa level ng kongreso.

**[01:06:03.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001250)  
And therefore, baka sakali mapagbibigyan po kami sa aming apilan na yan.

**[01:06:07.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001251)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[01:06:09.880] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001252)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Thank you, Chair.

**[01:06:11.880] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001253)  
Thank you, Representative Ega Erize.

**[01:06:14.880] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001254)  
Before we proceed, we would like to acknowledge the presence of Representative Iris Demesa Montes and

**[01:06:21.880] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001255)  
Vice Chair Isabella Maria Segar-Maria.

**[01:06:26.880] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001256)  
And also, Representative Saldivilla.

**[01:06:31.880] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001257)  
Next in line, from the majority, Representative Jonathan Keith Flores.

**[01:06:51.990] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001261)  
Yung BSKE po, Mr. Chairman,

**[01:06:52.990] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001262)  
Yung BSKE po, Mr. Chairman,

**[01:06:53.990] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001263)  
moore or less 12 billion pesos, pagkatapos po, ah yung pong nakaraan nung 2023.

**[01:06:59.990] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001264)

**[01:07:03.990] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001265)

**[01:07:09.990] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001266)

**[01:07:11.990] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001267)

**[01:07:16.990] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001269)

**[01:07:17.990] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001270)

**[01:07:22.990] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001271)

**[01:07:28.450] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001272)  
99.73 absorptive capacity, yet yung budget niyo

**[01:07:32.110] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001273)  
is binawasan ng almost 34%. Tama ba? Yes po,

**[01:07:37.270] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001274)

**[01:07:42.310] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001275)

**[01:14:44.700] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001462)  
25 na lang po, 2025

**[01:14:46.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001463)  
cases na lang po, ang natitira

**[01:14:48.700] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001464)  
sa amin. At sinabi niyo po that you had

**[01:14:50.660] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001465)  
62.47 case disposition.

**[01:14:53.100] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001466)  
Tama po ba?

**[01:14:54.920] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001467)  
Hindi lang po sa aming

**[01:14:56.740] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001468)  
clerk of the commission, kung hindi

**[01:14:59.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001469)  
po sa election.

**[01:15:00.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001470)  
Contest and Adjudication Department, kasama na rin po ang sa Law Department ng COVID.

**[01:15:04.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001471)  
Yung Law Department po namin medyo, hindi po ganun kabilis ang case disposition namin, Mr. Chair,

**[01:15:09.020] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001472)  
simply because we have to understand that cases before the Law Department are criminal in nature.

**[01:15:14.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001473)  
And therefore, we have to afford that highest degree of due process to all those respondents facing criminal prosecution.

**[01:15:22.060] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001474)  
Okay, now, hindi nagmamatch kasi. You said 14,000 cases, 62.47 case disposition.

**[01:15:30.000] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001475)  
And you said mga 400 lang yung naiwan. It should be about 5,000.

**[01:15:35.080] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001476)  
Meron po kasi mga nag-motion for reconsideration.

**[01:15:38.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001477)  
Therefore, yung pumbilang namin, because that is already still a disposition, kasama na rin po.

**[01:15:43.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001478)  
But the motion for reconsideration, that can easily be disposed already by the Commission and Bank.

**[01:15:47.840] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001479)  
Hindi naman kayo ma-affected itong case disposition ninyo with the budget that you'll be having?

**[01:15:54.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001480)  
Hindi naman po. We will definitely ensure this honorable body that case disposition will still proceed.

**[01:16:00.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001481)  
As is, regardless as to whether there will be a reduction in our budget.

**[01:16:04.440] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001482)  
Okay, now, the cases that you mentioned, ito ba ay parang initiated by other people?

**[01:16:09.420] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001483)  
Or are there cases that are more appropriate initiated by the Comelec?

**[01:16:12.840] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001484)  
Madami po dyan, especially sa Law Department, more appropriate initiated by the Political Finance and Affairs Department.

**[01:16:19.460] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001485)  
Especially yung mga donations. Especially yung mga overspending.

**[01:16:23.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001486)  
Madami po silang finile dyan.

**[01:16:25.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001487)  
Yung iba po, Mr. Chair, yung naabutan namin dati,

**[01:16:29.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001488)  
yung mga cases,

**[01:16:30.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001489)  
yung mga cases ng SK.

**[01:16:31.940] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001490)  
Alam nyo po, Mr. Chair, yung SK dati, before the 2023 elections,

**[01:16:36.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001491)  
ay 7,000 cases ang na-file sa sangguniang kabataan dahil over-aged po sila.

**[01:16:42.020] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001492)  
Magaling po yung mga iba eh.

**[01:16:43.420] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001493)  
Alam po nila na napakatagal ng disposition sa amin at that time,

**[01:16:47.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001494)  
so nagpa-file pa rin ng candidacy kahit sila ay more than 24 years of age.

**[01:16:51.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001495)  
Ngayon po, may solusyon kami.

**[01:16:52.860] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001496)  
Kaya po noong 2023, kumunti po ang nag-file.

**[01:16:55.540] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001497)  
Bakit po?

**[01:16:56.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001498)  
Hindi po namin tinatanggap yung certificates of candidacy ng mga kandidatasyon.

**[01:17:00.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001499)  
Kapag nakita namin sa record namin that they are more than 24 years of age kahit one day,

**[01:17:05.820] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001500)  
we will not accept anymore.

**[01:17:07.360] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001501)  
Ang sabi namin, if they want to go to the Supreme Court, then let them question.

**[01:17:10.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001502)  
Para lang po, hindi na 7,000 ang bilang.

**[01:17:13.480] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001503)  
Yung po mga kandidato for barangay candidates,

**[01:17:17.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001504)  
dati-dati po, kahit hindi registered voters, tinatanggap ni Comelec ang mga COC.

**[01:17:22.020] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001505)  
Ngayon po, pag nakita namin sa record namin na hindi pala registered voter doon sa barangay,

**[01:17:26.800] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001506)  
we will also not accept their certificates of candidacy.

**[01:17:30.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001512)  
So, yung mga innovations without requiring additional budget naman po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:17:34.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001513)  
So, may mga pamamaraan naman talaga kung magiging maayos lang sa procedure

**[01:17:39.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001514)  
basta may pag-observe ng due process.

**[01:17:42.780] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001515)  
Ito po.

**[01:17:43.320] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001516)  
But what happens if, example, 2022 pa, may overspending sa SOSE,

**[01:17:49.280] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001517)  
hindi pa na-actionan ng law department ninyo,

**[01:17:51.820] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001518)  
ay mag-pre-prescribe na iyan sa 2027, tama ba?

**[01:17:55.020] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001519)  
So long as we are able to start already the process,

**[01:17:58.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001520)  
wala pong hindi po mag-pre-prescribe yung five-year na prescriptive period.

**[01:18:05.860] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001521)  
Kasi yung five-year prescriptive period po is from the moment of the commission of the alleged election offense.

**[01:18:11.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001522)  
Kung walang kilos si Comelec, wala man lang pong initiation,

**[01:18:15.160] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001523)  
wala namang election, wala pong preliminary investigation,

**[01:18:18.100] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001524)  
then [unclear] po talaga. Mai-invoke po sa amin yung napakaraming desisyon ng Supreme Court

**[01:18:23.440] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001525)  
on the speedy disposition of cases.

**[01:18:26.300] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001526)  
But once nagsimula na po kami,

**[01:18:28.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001527)  
hindi na po mai-invoke sa

**[01:18:28.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001528)  
amin yung five years.

**[01:18:30.580] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001529)  
But these are supposedly matters that should have been initiated by the Komelik Motopropyo, di ba?

**[01:18:36.060] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001530)  
Kasi it's stated in the SOSE, di ba?

**[01:18:38.300] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001531)  
It's under oath, it's submitted to you.

**[01:18:40.380] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001532)  
Dapat kayo na po yung nag-initiate niyan, di ba?

**[01:18:42.700] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001533)  
Yes po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:18:43.480] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001534)  
In fact, we are going to submit to this committee, para lang din po maging transpire,

**[01:18:48.400] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001535)  
we are going to submit the list of cases filed by our Political Finance and Affairs Department

**[01:18:53.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001536)  
as Motopropyo initiated by that office without a need for anybody

**[01:18:57.540] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001537)  
filing a case.

**[01:19:00.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001538)  
Because po, kita po namin eh, madali po namin makompare.

**[01:19:04.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001539)  
There was one instance, for example, in Laguna.

**[01:19:06.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001540)  
Kinakita po namin kaagad noon na hindi siya nag-file doon sa SOSE niya.

**[01:19:12.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001541)  
Hindi siya nag-file nung kanyang ginastos.

**[01:19:15.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001542)  
Samantalang, for example, yung mismong station ng radyo or television,

**[01:19:20.420] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001543)  
nag-report, so kaya po namin makompare yun.

**[01:19:22.360] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001544)  
Even if, Mr. Chair, yung opisina po namin yun, yung PIFAD,

**[01:19:26.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001545)  
meron po siyang 48 na tao, lahat po yun, Mr. Chair, ay mga casual employees.

**[01:19:31.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001546)  
Wala po kaming regular office na yun.

**[01:19:34.220] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001547)  
And isa lang po ang aming, dalawa po ang lawyers namin, wala pong accountant doon.

**[01:19:38.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001548)  
They are able to dispose 48,000 SOSE in just a single election.

**[01:19:43.100] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001549)  
Thank you.

**[01:19:44.160] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001550)  
Mr. Chair, I would just like, before I leave, I would just like to manifest my support, of course,

**[01:19:48.460] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001551)  
for the restoration because of the dangers that we will be facing

**[01:19:53.040] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001552)  
if Comelec does not get at least a fair share.

**[01:19:56.000] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001553)  
of the budget that they are asking for.

**[01:19:58.160] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001554)  
But at the same time po, I hope that you will also try to resolve cases speedily po.

**[01:20:03.180] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001555)  
Kasi the thing I hate the most is when a case is filed, tapos muntik na mag-election,

**[01:20:09.100] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001556)  
tsaka pa mag-de-decide si Comelec.

**[01:20:11.780] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001557)  
So if we can avoid that and resolve things faster siguro, I would much appreciate it po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:20:17.900] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001558)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:20:18.540] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001559)  
We will submit, Mr. Chair, the list and then we will likewise submit to the Committee on Electoral Reforms

**[01:20:23.520] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001560)  
the updates on these cases.

**[01:20:24.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001561)  
Thank you very much.

**[01:20:26.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001562)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:20:27.000] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001563)  
Thank you, Representative Jonathan Kit Flores.

**[01:20:33.420] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001564)  
Next in line, from the minority, Representative Elijah San Fernando.

**[01:20:38.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001565)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:20:40.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001566)  
Chair Garcia, magandang hapon po sa Comelec family.

**[01:20:44.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001567)  
Meron lang po ilang mga paglilinaw na nais makuhang kinatawang ito.

**[01:20:48.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001568)  
Regarding po ito sa BARM elections,

**[01:20:51.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001569)  
just recently po, meron pong kinansila ng candidacy ang Comelec.

**[01:20:56.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001570)  
Ito po yung...

**[01:20:58.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001571)  
Candidacy ni Anwar Alamada.

**[01:21:02.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001572)  
Pupwede niyo po ba kaming bigyan ng briefer or ng background as to what led to the cancellation of his candidacy?

**[01:21:09.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001573)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[01:21:10.800] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001574)  
Without violating, because this is a hearing, without violating the rule on subjudice,

**[01:21:16.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001575)  
simply because we are fully aware that our decision will be elevated to the Supreme Court by the respondent,

**[01:21:23.020] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001576)  
the reason is because of the lack of residency.

**[01:21:26.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001577)  
Doon po kasi ay nagkaroon ng certification.

**[01:21:28.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001578)  
mismo ang barangay na siya ay hindi domiciled doon sa area.

**[01:21:34.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001579)  
May decision po tayo, three cases by the Supreme Court,

**[01:21:37.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001580)  
that the Comelec should give the highest consideration to a certification issued by the barangay,

**[01:21:44.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001581)  
being the one in charge of the place.

**[01:21:47.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001582)  
So yung po ang isa sa mga naging dahilan that to the belief of the Commission,

**[01:21:52.020] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001583)  
not only based on that but based on other evidence available,

**[01:21:55.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001584)  
he was not able to establish domicile in that place.

**[01:21:58.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001585)  
Thank you, Chair Garcia, doon po sa sagot niyo.

**[01:22:01.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001586)  
Kaya ako po ito na tanong, Mr. Chair, kasi hindi lang ito solely election issue.

**[01:22:07.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001587)  
This has something to do with the peace process happening in BARM.

**[01:22:12.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001588)  
Na alam naman po natin, kaya nagkakaroon ng eleksyon ngayon sa BARM,

**[01:22:16.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001589)  
ay dahil yan yung matagal na pangarap na ng mga kapatid nating Bangsamoro for self-determination.

**[01:22:22.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001590)  
So hindi po natin po pwedeng maipaghiwalay yung prosesong elektoral sa proseso ng usapingan,

**[01:22:28.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001591)  
yung pangkapayapaan.

**[01:22:30.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001592)  
Ngayon po, Chair Garcia, sa tingin niyo po ba, kasi ito pong si Mr. Anwar Alamada,

**[01:22:39.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001593)  
siya po ay isang senior MILF official, freedom fighter.

**[01:22:43.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001594)  
At alam naman po natin, bilang isang freedom fighter doon po sa conflict areas,

**[01:22:49.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001595)  
talagang wala siyang mapagtitirmisan na lugar kung saan siya nakatira.

**[01:22:58.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001596)  
Ngayon kinukonsidera ng Commission on Elections, itong nuances on the ongoing peace negotiations

**[01:23:05.480] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001597)  
and the rules na nag-govern dito po sa mga tumatakbong kandidato.

**[01:23:12.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001598)  
At yun po yung sinusunod ng Comelec.

**[01:23:13.920] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001599)  
Salamat po, Mr. Chair. Tama po ang inyong napuntos.

**[01:23:17.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001600)  
At kung yan man po ay gray area, Mr. Chair, yun din po ang kadahilanan kung bakit minarapat ni Comelec

**[01:23:23.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001601)  
na desisyonan ang kaso na ito.

**[01:23:25.500] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001602)  
Una po, Mr. Chair, to protect the interest of Mr. Alamada,

**[01:23:28.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001603)  
we did not remove his name sa balota.

**[01:23:31.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001604)  
Pinrint po namin yung kanyang pangalan sa balota.

**[01:23:34.100] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001605)  
Number two, hindi po namin sinagad ang paglalabas ng desisyon until September 14.

**[01:23:39.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001606)  
Pwede po sana namin gawin yun.

**[01:23:41.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001607)  
Ginawa po namin last week, nilabas ka agad namin yung desisyon

**[01:23:44.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001608)  
para meron po siyang sufficient time to go to the Honorable Supreme Court.

**[01:23:48.920] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001609)  
So that kung if people will still be voting for him,

**[01:23:52.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001610)  
or especially if he is able to get a restraining order,

**[01:23:54.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001611)  
then at least po, Mr. Chair,

**[01:23:56.860] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001612)  
lahat po ng voto niya will be considered,

**[01:23:58.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001613)  
valid at hindi po siya makukonsider na stray votes.

**[01:24:02.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001614)  
At least binigyan po namin ng panahon siya.

**[01:24:05.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001615)  
At least po, yung more than two weeks na yan,

**[01:24:07.820] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001616)  
hopefully po makapunta siya sa Korte Suprema.

**[01:24:09.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001617)  
Number three, talaga pong sana because of this case,

**[01:24:13.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001618)  
maging opportunity po ito sa ating kataas-taasang hukuman

**[01:24:17.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001619)  
upang malinaw yung mga ganyang bagay.

**[01:24:20.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001620)  
For example, tama po yung binabanggit ng inyong,

**[01:24:22.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001621)  
your honor, ng inyo po.

**[01:24:26.160] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001622)  
Paano yung halimbawa po yung ating mga kababayan,

**[01:24:28.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001623)  
na peripatetic, yung bang meron siyang,

**[01:24:31.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001624)  
meron po siya ay nangangalaka lang,

**[01:24:34.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001625)  
pagkatapos ay napupunta sa bawat bayan,

**[01:24:37.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001626)  
saan mo siya kukonsider na domicile?

**[01:24:40.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001627)  
Tama po kayo, walang domicile yung technically eh,

**[01:24:43.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001628)  
but we have to understand that situation.

**[01:24:45.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001629)  
Our problem po talaga lang, Mr. Chair,

**[01:24:47.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001630)  
is because of the requirements of the Local Government Code,

**[01:24:51.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001631)  
of the requirements of the Bangsamoro Electoral Code,

**[01:24:54.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001632)  
and of the consistent decision of the Supreme Court

**[01:24:56.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001633)  
na it comes to domicile.

**[01:24:57.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001634)  
Thank you.

**[01:24:58.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001635)  
So, sana nga po, mag-grab itong opportunity ito,

**[01:25:01.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001636)  
maliwanag talaga ito, to protect the interest,

**[01:25:04.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001637)  
not only of Mr. Alamada, but likewise,

**[01:25:06.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001638)  
those who will be facing the same predicament in the future.

**[01:25:09.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001639)  
Maraming salamat po, Chair Garcia, dun po sa sagot ninyo.

**[01:25:13.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001640)  
Maaaring makatulong po ito para doon sa pagresolba

**[01:25:17.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001641)  
sa mga ganitong usapin at sa mga susunod pang usapin.

**[01:25:20.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001642)  
Mr. Chair, kaya ako lang naman po ito binibigyang din,

**[01:25:23.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001643)  
dahil mahalaga po na maipasok sa usapin ng eleksyon sa BARM,

**[01:25:27.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001644)  
ulit yung ongoing na peace negotiation

**[01:25:30.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001645)  
between the Government of the Republic of the Philippines

**[01:25:33.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001646)  
and the Bangsamoro people.

**[01:25:35.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001647)  
So, paramount po kasi yung usapin ng self-determination.

**[01:25:39.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001648)  
So, yun po ang tinitindigan natin na mapagpas siya dito sa usapin ito.

**[01:25:44.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001649)  
Pero, I am satisfied po sa sagot po ni Chair Garcia.

**[01:25:47.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001650)  
Maraming salamat po, Chair, at sa buong Comelec family.

**[01:25:50.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001651)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Chair, your Honor.

**[01:25:52.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001652)  
Thank you very much po.

**[01:25:53.620] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001653)  
Thank you, Kong Eli.

**[01:25:55.620] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001654)  
Next in line,

**[01:25:57.620] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-001655)  
Representative Atty. JP Padiernos.

**[01:25:59.620] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001656)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:26:01.620] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001657)  
Magandang hapon po sa ating kagalang-galang na chairperson,

**[01:26:06.620] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001658)  
Chair Garcia, and the rest of the Comelec family.

**[01:26:10.620] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001659)  
So, may mga ano lang po ako concerned,

**[01:26:13.620] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001660)  
or mga questions po, katanungan, Mr. Chair.

**[01:26:16.620] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001661)  
Unahin ko muna po yung ating barangay NSK elections po sa November.

**[01:26:23.620] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001662)  
Kasi po,

**[01:26:25.620] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001663)  
ah,

**[01:26:27.970] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001664)  
according to Comelec, sinabi po nila, sinabi na around,

**[01:26:33.970] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001665)  
out of, around 8 billion of approximately 19 billion of the BSKE budget had already been spent.

**[01:26:42.970] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001666)  
With most of the balance intended for poll worker honorary training and development,

**[01:26:47.970] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001667)  
and another 2.5 to 3.5 billion could be required pag na-postpone po.

**[01:26:54.970] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001668)  
Ano na po ba yung status ngayon po dun sa,

**[01:26:55.970] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001669)  
preparation po, at yung ina-mention niyo po na 8 billion na nag-gastos niya na po?

**[01:27:01.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001670)  
Ah, lahat po ng mga kagamitan na gagamitin natin para sa barangay NSK elections noong November 2 ay nakahanda na.

**[01:27:08.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001671)  
Lalong-lalo na po yung mga balota.

**[01:27:10.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001672)  
In fact, yung pong mga dagdag na balota, dahil nagkaroon po tayo ng dagdag na botante,

**[01:27:15.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001673)  
nagkaroon po tayo ng registration from October 20 to May 18 ng 2020, ah, 2027, ah, 2026,

**[01:27:24.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001674)  
and therefore, nagkaroon po tayo ng dagdag na, hindi po muna namin i-imprinta yun,

**[01:27:29.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001675)  
ah, pero later on, ma-i-imprinta na namin.

**[01:27:31.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001676)  
Yung pong mga iba pang dapat mag-procure, hindi po muna namin pinadeliver, pero nabili na rin po namin.

**[01:27:37.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001677)  
So, ibig sabihin po, anytime, kahit po magpahold tayo ng election by next week, pwede namin isagawa ang barangay NSK.

**[01:27:44.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001678)  
Ang kulang po namin lang na major, as number one, yung po training ng mga guro.

**[01:27:48.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001679)  
We'll have to train the more or less 1 million teachers, ah, the members of the electoral board,

**[01:27:53.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001680)  
including the support staff, kasi po manual na election ang barangay NSK.

**[01:27:57.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001681)  
And therefore, pati po yung mga DESO. And therefore, kailangan po namin ma-train sila.

**[01:28:02.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001682)  
At ah, dahil po dyan, yun na lang po yung critical na kulang ni Commission on Election.

**[01:28:07.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001683)  
Yung ballot boxes po, nakapag-procure kami ng dagdag na 70,000.

**[01:28:11.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001684)  
At anytime, pwede po namin pa-deliver yun, tutal bakal naman po yung mga ballot boxes na yan.

**[01:28:16.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001685)  
Yun nga lang po, yung deployment, yun na lang po hinihintay natin para ma-deploy yung mga kagamitan natin.

**[01:28:23.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001686)  
Ah, kasi kaya po nagpasabi po ang inyong lingkod sa ating pong Kongreso,

**[01:28:28.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001687)  
nung magkaroon ng pagdinig dito po at sa mataas na kapulungan sa Senado,

**[01:28:32.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001688)  
na baka po pwede naman bago po matapos ang buwan ng September,

**[01:28:35.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001689)  
ay maging maliwanag kung talagang matutuloy o hindi ang halalan.

**[01:28:39.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001690)  
At ah, ang nangyari naman po sa Senado kasi ay hindi po siya, ah, postponement

**[01:28:47.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001691)  
kung hindi po, ah, fixing of the term from four years to five years.

**[01:28:52.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001692)  
At dito po sa mababang kapulungan, nagkaroon na rin po ng pagdinig nung nakaraang linggo,

**[01:28:57.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001693)  
ay term fixing na rin po ang dininig po ng local government committee.

**[01:29:03.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001694)  
So yan po yung hinihintay namin sa kasalukuyan.

**[01:29:06.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001695)  
So hopefully po, hanggang before third week na September, baka po maging maliwanag.

**[01:29:10.970] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001696)  
Mr. Chair, follow-up question po.

**[01:29:12.970] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001697)  
Doon sa 8 billion po na nagastos na po, how much was merely obligated versus magkano po yung paid and delivered?

**[01:29:21.970] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001698)  
Tapos in addition po, ito po bang na-procure nyo na or nagastos no?

**[01:29:27.970] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001699)  
Can it still be used for next year's election if it will be postponed next year?

**[01:29:33.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001700)  
More or less po, Mr. Chair, ang earmark natin is 4 billion.

**[01:29:36.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001701)  
At ang ating naman din pong obligated, more or less din po, 3.9, 4 billion na rin po halos ang obligated natin.

**[01:29:43.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001702)  
So all in all po, 8 billion, 3 million ang atin pong obligated at saka earmark na amount.

**[01:29:51.970] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001704)  
At yan naman po, para po doon sa mga nakikinig at nakamonitor sa atin, lahat naman po yun ay magagamit.

**[01:30:00.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001705)  
kung sakaling hindi matuloy ang ating halalan sa 26 November 2.

**[01:30:08.560] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001706)  
Okay po. So thank you po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:30:12.040] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001707)  
On my second point, kasi po nagkaroon po recently ng election sa Das Marinas in Cavite.

**[01:30:22.400] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001708)  
And according to news article in your report,

**[01:30:26.120] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001709)  
from your supposed 200 million budget, napababa niyo po ng 128 million.

**[01:30:34.820] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001710)  
Plus, meron pa pong contribution yung ating LGU na around 50 million po.

**[01:30:42.340] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001711)  
Tama po ba yan, Mr. Chair?

**[01:30:44.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001712)  
Tama po kayo, Mr. Chair.

**[01:30:46.300] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001713)  
Napakalaking tulong po yung in advance sa amin po ng local government unit ng Das Marinas Cavite.

**[01:30:52.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001714)  
Gusto ko lang din pong sabihin for record purposes.

**[01:30:56.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001715)  
Na ang omnibus election code under Section 10 ay nagsasabi na si Comelec,

**[01:31:00.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001716)  
and that is a 1985 law.

**[01:31:03.100] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001717)  
Whether we agree with that or not, it's a 1985 law.

**[01:31:05.840] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001718)  
Sabi po, the Comelec can ask the local government unit to advance expenses that will be incurred

**[01:31:12.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001719)  
for purposes of the conduct of any special election.

**[01:31:15.100] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001720)  
Yung po yung ginawa sa atin.

**[01:31:16.760] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001721)  
At this point po, Mr. Chair, hindi naman po namin po mababayaran o maibabalik sa LGU

**[01:31:21.440] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001722)  
sapagkat kinakailangan po muna may pumasok na savings.

**[01:31:24.500] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001723)  
E base po sa desisyon.

**[01:31:26.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001724)  
Ang Supreme Court sa atin sa DAP cases, savings can only be incurred kapag po natapos na ang year.

**[01:31:32.420] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001725)  
And therefore, sa ngayon, wala pa po kami savings para maibalik.

**[01:31:35.520] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001726)  
Napakalaking tulong po yun sapagkat ginamit po namin yung 50 million sa pagpapabigay ng onorarya sa ating mga guro.

**[01:31:42.480] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001727)  
And for the information likewise of the body, for the second time in the history of the Commission,

**[01:31:47.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001728)  
on the night of the election, nabayaran po namin ang lahat ng mga guro na nagsilbi sa election.

**[01:31:52.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001729)  
Una na po ang antipolo dito po sa Das Marinas.

**[01:31:56.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001730)  
Hindi po kami naghintay ng 15 days.

**[01:31:58.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001731)  
On the same night, nagbayad na rin po kami sa mga guro.

**[01:32:01.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001732)  
So again, that is still subject to reimbursement.

**[01:32:04.460] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001733)  
Yan naman po yung exact wordings ng batas natin.

**[01:32:07.520] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001734)  
That's very good to hear.

**[01:32:09.020] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001735)  
Actually, hindi rin po aware na it was an advance.

**[01:32:13.820] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001736)  
Meaning, babayaran nyo ulit, pabalik.

**[01:32:17.760] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001737)  
Tama po yan.

**[01:32:18.640] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001738)  
Tama po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:32:20.440] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001739)  
But is it common practice po?

**[01:32:22.280] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001740)  
I know you mentioned na nasa batas po yan.

**[01:32:24.580] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001741)  
Pero, is that common?

**[01:32:26.120] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001742)  
Is it common practice na ang LGU, pag nagkakaroon po ng vacancy, nag-a-advance po?

**[01:32:33.780] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001743)  
And wala po bang, siyempre may mga nag-raise po ng issue na since yung tumakbo is part of the LGU,

**[01:32:42.320] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001744)  
there's some sort of conflict of interest.

**[01:32:45.080] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001745)  
Can you, ano po, expand?

**[01:32:46.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001746)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[01:32:47.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001747)  
Actually po, may mga nag-question, especially sa social media.

**[01:32:51.160] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001748)  
Lalo na po sa part ng local government unit, hindi naman po sa part ni Comel.

**[01:32:55.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001749)  
Kasi, sinabi ko,

**[01:32:56.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001750)  
sabi namin, ang pag-gastos ng kahit anong item, kami ang may final say, kami ang may desisyon,

**[01:33:01.820] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001751)  
hindi yung nagbigay na local government unit.

**[01:33:04.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001752)  
Ito po yung pangatlong pagkakataon na ang Comelic ay nabigyan ng advance.

**[01:33:08.420] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001753)  
Katulad po, yan nangyari na po yung sa Campostela Valley,

**[01:33:11.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001754)  
nagkaroon din po ng ganyang special election.

**[01:33:14.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001755)  
At nangyari na rin po ito, dyan po sa Cavite,

**[01:33:17.440] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001756)  
nung pong mabakantihan ang posisyon ni dating congressman,

**[01:33:21.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001757)  
ngayon ay ombudsman, Boying Remulia.

**[01:33:24.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001758)  
Nung siya po yung matalaga.

**[01:33:26.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001759)  
Bilang Secretary of Justice, kami po ay tinulungan din ng Provincial Government of Cavite

**[01:33:31.220] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001760)  
at kami po ay nakapagbalik naman ng pondo rin po sa kanila.

**[01:33:34.640] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001761)  
So, hindi po ito extraordinary or unusual sapagkat nasa batas.

**[01:33:41.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001762)  
Sabi nga po namin, maaaring hindi lang nari-research ng lahat yung provision po yun ng batas.

**[01:33:47.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001763)  
Ang pinaka-importante po, Mr. Chair, yung desisyon ng Korte Suprema

**[01:33:50.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001764)  
sa Hagedorn versus the House of Representatives.

**[01:33:54.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001765)  
Sa pagkat, nagkaroon po nang,

**[01:33:56.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001766)  
nagpagbabago ng prinsipyo at ang mismong Korte Suprema.

**[01:34:00.800] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001767)  
Nung una po, si Comelec ay hindi mag-hold ng kahit na anong special election.

**[01:34:05.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001768)  
Kahit pa may namatay, o nag-resign, o natanggal na membro ng House of Representatives.

**[01:34:11.640] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001769)  
Kung walang, number one, declaration of vacancy by the House of Representatives.

**[01:34:16.300] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001770)  
Number two, call for a special election.

**[01:34:18.420] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001771)  
Kung natatandaan po natin, Mr. Chair,

**[01:34:20.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001772)  
at ang tinangga po ni Secretary Gatchalian, Rex Gatchalian, ang posisyon ng DSWD.

**[01:34:28.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001773)  
Dati po siyang Congresswoman ng Valenzuela.

**[01:34:31.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001774)  
Subalit, hindi po nagkaroon na special election sa pagkat ng congressman

**[01:34:35.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001775)  
sapagkat hindi po nagpatawag ang kongreso at that time.

**[01:34:39.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001776)  
Namatay po si Congressman Salvame ng Palawan.

**[01:34:42.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001777)  
Hindi po nagpatawag ang mababang kapulungan

**[01:34:45.500] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001778)  
pero diniklara po ng mababang kapulungan o vacant ang posisyon.

**[01:34:49.400] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001779)  
Dito po sa kaso ni Haguidoin ay hindi po kami nag-hold ng election,

**[01:34:55.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001780)  
ng special election, sapagkat wala pong deklarasyon ng House of Representatives.

**[01:34:58.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001781)  
Wala rin pong pagpapatawag ng special election.

**[01:35:02.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001782)  
Subalit, dinala po ng pamilya ng yumaong Congressman Haguidoin

**[01:35:05.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001783)  
ang issue sa Korte Suprema.

**[01:35:07.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001784)  
At ang sabi ng Korte Suprema, number one,

**[01:35:09.700] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001785)  
hindi kinakailangang umasa ni Comelec

**[01:35:11.420] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-001786)  
sa kahit na anong declaration of vacancy by the House of Representatives.

**[01:45:36.220] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002086)

**[01:45:40.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002088)

**[01:45:42.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002089)

**[01:45:54.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002095)

**[01:45:58.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002097)

**[01:46:09.040] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002102)

**[01:46:11.160] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002103)

**[01:46:19.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002107)

**[01:46:22.640] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002109)  
pag-internet voting. Ano po ang naging issue nung nakaraan?

**[01:46:25.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002110)  
First time po yon. Kaya, alam namin,

**[01:46:27.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002111)  
may mga lalabas na,

**[01:46:28.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002112)  
issue, which is okay lang po. Ang issue po

**[01:46:30.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002113)

**[01:46:34.220] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002115)

**[01:46:36.020] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002116)

**[01:46:39.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002117)

**[01:46:40.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002118)

**[01:46:41.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002119)

**[01:46:43.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002120)

**[01:46:48.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002122)

**[01:46:50.160] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002123)

**[01:46:53.300] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002124)  
Pwede nyo po, pwede

**[01:46:54.080] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002125)

**[01:46:55.160] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002126)

**[01:47:01.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002129)

**[01:47:04.320] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002130)

**[01:47:06.640] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002131)

**[01:47:08.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002132)

**[01:47:10.100] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002133)

**[01:47:12.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002134)

**[01:47:14.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002135)

**[01:47:16.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002136)

**[01:47:18.420] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002137)

**[01:47:20.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002138)

**[01:47:23.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002139)  
Kasi po, Mr. Chair,

**[01:47:25.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002140)

**[01:47:26.840] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002141)

**[01:47:28.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002142)

**[01:47:30.800] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002143)

**[01:47:33.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002144)

**[01:47:35.100] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002145)

**[01:47:37.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002146)

**[01:47:38.460] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002147)

**[01:47:41.320] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002148)

**[01:47:43.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002149)

**[01:47:46.800] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002151)

**[01:47:48.920] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002152)

**[01:47:51.040] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002153)

**[01:47:58.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002157)

**[01:48:03.080] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002159)

**[01:48:04.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002160)

**[01:48:06.940] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002161)

**[01:48:09.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002162)

**[01:51:20.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002253)

**[01:51:27.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002256)  
ng mga teachers.

**[01:51:30.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002257)  
Of course,

**[01:51:31.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002258)  
as of now,

**[01:51:33.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002259)  
ay,

**[01:51:34.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002260)

**[01:51:46.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002262)

**[01:52:02.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002263)  
So para malinaw po, magkano po ba ang meron kayong panukala? Magkano na yung panukala natin ng mga increase para sa election service workers natin?

**[01:52:14.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002264)
