**[01:52:19.100] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002265)  
Meaning to say, ngayon po ang chairman of the board ay 12,000, ang member vice chair ay 11,000 at yung third member ay 10,000 pesos.

**[01:52:27.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002266)  
Iba pa po yung honoraria ng ating deso at yung mga support staff.

**[01:52:32.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002267)  
Ninagdagan din po natin yun ng additional 2,000 pesos.

**[01:52:36.040] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002268)  
Naniniwala po tayo, tama po, because of the crisis that we are experiencing right now, talaga naman po kailangan ng mga guro ng dagdag.

**[01:52:43.520] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002269)  
Kaya yung pinopropose po, masyado lang po talagang malaki yung 15,000 pesos per teacher.

**[01:52:49.700] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002270)  
Siguro po yung 15,000 sa chairman, 14,000 sa vice chairman at saka 13,000 sa miembro.

**[01:52:55.640] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002271)  
That's also, that's very reasonable kung tutuusin po.

**[01:52:58.020] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002272)  
Mabigyan lang po tayo ng budget.

**[01:52:59.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002273)  
Kasi naman po, mas mahirap ang barangay ng SK election, Mr. Chair.

**[01:53:03.760] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002274)  
Bakit po mas mahirap? Binibilang po manually ang balota.

**[01:53:07.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002275)  
Doon nga po sa Dasmariñas na isa lang ang kandidato, ang isa lang ang mananalo at lima ang kandidato,

**[01:53:15.760] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002276)  
inabot na rin po sila na mga hanggang 9 o'clock ng gabi at magteteng pa nga po bago ma-deliver yung mga gamit.

**[01:53:22.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002277)  
So meaning to say, paano pa po sa isang barangay ng SK na may SK ka na may barangay ka pa.

**[01:53:27.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002278)  
So abutin po eh na hanggang...

**[01:53:29.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002279)  
Tapos iba pa po yung pag-deliver.

**[01:53:31.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002280)  
So kami po, sumusuporta kami sa mataas, sa pagtataas ng onorarya po ng ating mga...

**[01:53:36.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002281)  
Yung pong ating pong service incentive leave na napakatagal ng batas na naan dyan, 5 days.

**[01:53:41.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002282)  
Ginawa po natin 10 days sa pamagitan din po ng pagsususog ng ACT-T Church.

**[01:53:46.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002283)  
Ginawa po natin 10 araw yan.

**[01:53:48.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002284)  
At ngayon po, ginawa na rin po na inyong commission na only na po yun.

**[01:53:52.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002285)  
Wala pong expiry date.

**[01:53:54.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002286)  
Hindi po katulad ng dati na expired lang siya or can be used only within the same year.

**[01:53:59.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002287)  
Kung saan po na-entitled yung mga guru natin.

**[01:54:02.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002288)  
Okay. Ngayon, so...

**[01:54:04.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002289)  
Again, Chair.

**[01:54:05.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002290)  
Kung matuloy yung eleksyon, magkano ang budget para sa onorarium?

**[01:54:13.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002291)  
Noong po kasing nagbigay ng dagdag na 1,000 pesos ang ating Pangulo, natatandaan po natin noong nakaraang halalan,

**[01:54:19.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002292)

**[01:54:26.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002293)  
So more or less po kung hanggang 15,000 niya.

**[01:54:28.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002294)  
Sa ating pong palagay, mga hanggang 2 to 2.5 billion pesos.

**[01:54:34.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002295)  
Okay. So kailangan natin ng...

**[01:54:37.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002296)  
Para mataas natin to 15,000 para sa chair ng BEI at 14 para sa mga members and so on,

**[01:54:46.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002297)  
ay kailangan nyo ng kabuoang 2.5, 2 to 2.5 billion pesos.

**[01:54:54.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002298)

**[01:54:55.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002299)  
Yes po. Yes po, Mr. Chair.

**[01:54:57.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002300)  
Para lang po sa increase in the onorary of the teachers.

**[01:55:01.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002301)

**[01:55:06.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002302)

**[01:55:11.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002303)

**[01:55:18.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002304)  
Lalo pa, ang position natin, malinaw, dapat matuloy ang eleksyon, pero kung di matuloy ang barangay election at ma-postpone,

**[01:55:27.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002305)  
ay haharapin pa rin itong usapin ng onorarium ng mga teachers.

**[01:55:31.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002306)

**[01:55:41.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002307)  
So kung maaari po ay magbigay po sa committee nung magkano yun. May binanggit kayo 2 to 2.5 billion. Ipirmina po natin kung maaari po.

**[01:55:51.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002308)  
Yes po, Mr. Chair. In fact, sa 2027 budget proposal, ilalagay na po namin yun.

**[01:55:56.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002309)  
Para mali bang po sa sulat namin sa committee on appropriation, kahit sa Committee on Suffrage and Electoral Reforms,

**[01:56:03.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002310)  
lalagay na rin po natin yan sa proposal so that pag nag-defend kami ng budget sa DBM, that will now be included.

**[01:56:10.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002311)  
At sana po masuportahan kami doon, doon sa nagdag na onoraryo natin sa mga guro.

**[01:56:15.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002312)  
Okay, so thank you. Yan lang po. Sana po maisubmit nyo na sa committee para matiyak, I am sure, susuportahan po ng ating Vice Chair.

**[01:56:28.660] Rep. Keith Micah "Atty. Mike" D.L. Tan** (asr-002314)

**[01:56:35.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002316)

**[01:57:55.860] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002325)

**[01:58:05.860] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002326)

**[01:58:14.860] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-002327)

**[02:23:41.160] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003052)

**[02:23:45.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003054)

**[02:23:47.400] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003055)

**[02:23:59.740] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003061)

**[02:24:01.520] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003062)

**[02:24:13.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003068)

**[02:24:15.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003069)

**[02:24:31.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003077)

**[02:24:33.440] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003078)

**[02:24:38.640] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003080)

**[02:24:39.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003081)

**[02:24:51.940] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003087)

**[02:26:17.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003130)

**[02:26:19.760] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003131)

**[02:26:27.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003135)

**[02:26:29.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003136)

**[02:26:41.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003142)  
ng anti-political dynasty

**[02:26:43.480] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003143)  
for national or local position.

**[02:26:46.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003144)  
That the Comelec cannot motopropio

**[02:26:48.160] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003145)

**[02:26:50.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003146)

**[02:26:51.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003147)

**[02:26:53.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003148)

**[02:26:55.700] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003149)

**[02:26:57.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003150)

**[02:26:59.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003151)  
There should be a petition. So,

**[02:27:01.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003152)  
yan po, critical na

**[02:27:03.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003153)

**[02:27:05.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003154)

**[02:27:07.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003155)

**[02:27:13.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003158)

**[02:27:15.840] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003159)

**[02:27:17.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003160)

**[02:27:19.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003161)

**[02:27:21.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003162)

**[02:27:23.840] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003163)

**[02:27:29.820] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003166)

**[02:27:31.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003167)

**[02:27:33.920] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003168)

**[02:27:45.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003174)

**[02:28:46.320] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003204)  
George Garcia

**[02:28:54.980] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003208)

**[02:28:58.980] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003210)

**[02:29:00.280] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003211)

**[02:29:04.660] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003213)

**[02:29:06.580] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003214)

**[02:29:09.140] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003215)

**[02:29:10.620] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003216)

**[02:29:13.140] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003217)

**[02:29:14.420] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003218)

**[02:29:16.860] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003219)

**[02:29:18.600] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003220)

**[02:29:20.380] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003221)

**[02:29:23.720] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003222)

**[02:29:24.840] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003223)

**[02:29:26.940] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003224)

**[02:29:28.980] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003225)

**[02:29:31.220] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003226)

**[02:29:33.280] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003227)

**[02:29:34.860] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003228)

**[02:29:37.160] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003229)

**[02:29:39.060] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003230)

**[02:29:41.840] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003231)

**[02:29:43.140] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003232)  
ay since

**[02:29:45.380] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003233)

**[02:29:46.700] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003234)  
House Bill na ito, ano po

**[02:29:49.500] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003235)

**[02:29:51.580] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003236)

**[02:29:53.460] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003237)  
ng Kongreso

**[02:29:54.960] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003238)  
o matapos

ang lahat ng prosesong pagdadaanan

**[02:29:58.980] Rep. Ramil L. Hernandez** (asr-003240)  
ito para po ito ay

**[02:30:08.100] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003245)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[02:30:09.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003246)  
Sana po before the end of the year.

**[02:30:11.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003247)  
Dahil po, siyempre, magre-resist ang Kongreso

**[02:30:13.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003248)  
ng first week ng December, magre-resist

**[02:30:15.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003249)  
ang Kongreso ng October, tapos

**[02:30:17.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003250)  
pagkatapos po, magre-resist po ang Kongreso

**[02:30:19.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003251)  
muli ng March.

**[02:30:21.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003252)  
And therefore, filing na po tayo ng candidacy

**[02:30:23.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003253)  
pagdating po ng third week

**[02:30:25.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003254)  
of September sa 2027.

**[02:30:28.640] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003255)  
So talaga pong critical

**[02:30:29.700] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003256)  
na sana ma-approve natin ito

**[02:30:31.860] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003257)  
before the end of the year.

**[02:30:33.920] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003258)  
Alam niyo po, Mr. Chair, napaka-crucial

**[02:30:36.040] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003259)  
na bang buti po nabanggit yung bagay na yan

**[02:30:37.860] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003260)  
sapagkat sa recent decisions

**[02:30:40.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003261)  
po ng Supreme Court, sana po

**[02:30:41.940] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003262)  
ma-review na rin natin ang definition ng who is

**[02:30:43.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003263)  
a nuisance candidate

**[02:30:46.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003264)  
under Section 69 ng

**[02:30:47.820] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003265)  
Omnibus Election Code. Bakit po?

**[02:30:50.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003266)  
Sapagkat halos wala na po kaming

**[02:30:51.800] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003267)  
diskresyon sa pag-disqualify.

**[02:30:53.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003268)  
Una, hindi kami pwede mag-disqualify

**[02:30:55.480] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003269)  
basta may political party. Kahit anong political party

**[02:30:58.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003270)  
yan, basta meron, hindi siya

**[02:30:59.520] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003271)  
nuisans. Hindi kami pwede mag-disqualify

**[02:31:01.480] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003272)  
dahil makukunti lang ang nakuhan niyang voto

**[02:31:03.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003273)  
noong nakaraan. Hindi kami pwede mag-disqualify

**[02:31:05.640] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003274)  
dahil kaparehas lang siya ng pangalan

**[02:31:07.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003275)  
ng isang kandidato. Hindi kami pwede

**[02:31:09.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003276)  
mag-disqualify dahil lang sa kulang

**[02:31:11.700] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003277)  
siya sa pondo, wala siyang financial capacity

**[02:31:13.820] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003278)

**[02:31:15.800] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003279)  
naman siya, ikukunti ang supporters niya

**[02:31:17.860] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003280)  
o hindi siya well-organized. Eh wala na po

**[02:31:19.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003281)  
kaming ground to disqualify based on

**[02:31:21.640] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003282)  
reasons. Inaalala nga po namin ngayon

**[02:31:23.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003283)  
sa Comelec, paano kung may mga

**[02:31:25.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003284)  
kandidato tayo for president, halimbawa

**[02:31:27.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003285)  
mga 60.

**[02:31:29.520] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003286)  
Eh practically po, wala na kami

**[02:31:30.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003287)  
mag-disqualify o maka-cancel dahil po

**[02:31:32.840] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003288)  
doon sa bagay na yun. Eh kung magkakaganon

**[02:31:35.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003289)  
po, 60 po ang pangalan na nakalagay

**[02:31:37.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003290)  
na kandidato natin sa balota,

**[02:31:39.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-003291)  
ganun po kadami,

**[03:00:00.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004139)  
Sa bandang huli po, hindi po ba kahit sa Republic Act 7160, yung ating local government code, hindi po ba ang SK naman, hindi pwedeng gumasta ng kahit anong pondo, nang hindi po otorisado at under the control and supervision ng barangay.

**[03:00:17.080] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004140)  
So yung sinasabing hindi nagagasos ng tama, ay di dapat din po nabiblaim din pala si barangay chairman sapagkat lahat po ng apaggasos ng SK ay may imprimatur ng barangay chairman at sa kabarangay council.

**[03:00:29.360] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004141)  
So meaning to say po, hindi po ito kasalanan lang ng kabataan. Madami po magagawa dahil ang kabataan nagiging parte nung nalalaman po nila kung paano pinapamahalaan ang bayan, paano inaayos ang paggasos ng pondo.

**[03:00:43.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004142)  
Kung meron po ilan na nagiging palpak o nagiging bulok, hindi po dapat mablaim yung karamihan sapagkat nung kabataan barangay pa lang maayos naman po eh. Ito pong sangguni ang kabataan maayos din naman po.

**[03:00:58.360] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004143)

**[03:01:57.220] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004148)

**[03:02:19.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004150)

**[03:02:42.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004153)

Hindi po yung naglalakad sila mula doon sa bundok, papunta sa kapatagan. But just the same, hindi pa rin po sapat.

**[03:05:35.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004170)

**[03:05:57.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004171)

**[03:06:21.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004172)

Sinisigurado po namin na nakakaboto sila kahit sa Bureau of Corrections at saka po sa BJMP ay makakaboto po sila. So para po yang lahat ng yan ay mapatunayan natin na inclusiveness ay pinakaimportante sa lahat. Wala po tayong dapat iiwan.

**[03:06:42.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004173)  
Meron po tayong mga lugar dati, kahit yung kapihan na naging sikat dati sa Surigao, pinuntahan po namin yun. First time, 80 years old, 85 years old, nagpaparehistro po sila. Mula sa Mindoro, sa Bukidnon, sa Tarlac, sa Zambales, kahit po sa Pampanga and such other places and Palawan. So, pero po madami pa rin po tayong mga kababayan na kinakailangang ma-reach ng komisyon. Kung kaya nga po yung pagtanggal sa amin ng budget na katulad na nabanggit ni Congresswoman Elago kanina, malaking bagay po yun sapagkat baka hindi na po namin sila basta marating dahil dito sa pagkaltas ng budget ng Commission on Elections. But ipinapangako po namin sa inyo, we will never leave anybody unregistered because disenfranchisement is never the business of this republic. Disenfranchisement is never the business of the Commission on Elections. Again, maraming salamat po.

At yan po yung aking mga advocacy in many, many proposals before the separation and electoral.

**[03:09:28.740] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004177)  
Ngayon po, nung nag-petition po ako against Miru nung nakaraan dahil sinabi ko prototype yan, bago yan, nakakatakot yan, at nung namang nag-work sinabi ko, okay, napatunayan ko. I've been a member of the Joint Congressional Committee on Automation for 12 years at marami naman po tayong experience diyan. Ngayon po gusto ko lang pong, ano, I just would like to get the real frame na mindset of the COMELEC and the COMELEC Advisory Council on the use of machines.

**[03:09:58.740] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004178)  
Kasi po, as you said kanina, ang mindset niyo, brand new. Ang para sa akin, the COMELEC should rather specify, rather than brand new, yung performance standards: accuracy, reliability, security,

**[03:10:30.180] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004179)  
auditability and hindi capacity to upgrade. Yun, mas tingin ko po, mas yun ang importante kaysa sa brand new. Second, sa experience ko sa automation, kung gagamitin natin yung nakaraan, palagay ko yung problema niyo na 9 billion na kulang baka konti na lang. At makakatipid yung mga Pilipino,

**[03:11:00.180] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004180)  
makakatipid yung ating bansa sa isang sistema na napatunayan na. But I would like to give you the benefit of the doubt. So ganito po, hihingi lang po ako ng kopya nung bago ninyong terms of reference. Hingi po ako ng kopya kung may minutes of the meeting or resolution yung COMELEC Advisory Council para mapag-aralan ko po. And I assure that I will be very objective. Titignan ko kung necessary

**[03:11:30.180] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-004181)  
yung bago o whimsical, at sasabihin ko po sa inyo sa plenary based on aking pag-aaral. Kaya po hinihiling ko na mabigyan niyo ako ng kopya before the plenary debates on the GAA. Salamat po.

**[03:11:46.840] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004181-split-2)  
Yes po, Mr. Chair. We will definitely comply. Kasama na rin po yung terms of reference ng transmission at terms of reference ng OVCS po natin. Kasi po yung mga yan ay unbundled lahat and therefore, magkakaiba po ang terms of reference ng bawat isa.

**[03:14:51.580] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004187)  
First regular parliamentary elections. Historic siya dahil ang setup po sa BARM is parliamentary.

**[03:15:00.000] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004188)  
So ito po yung magtataglay doon sa katibayan na naging resulta nung usapang pangkapayapaan.

**[03:15:10.820] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004189)  
So we'd like to, for the chairman, to update this committee.

**[03:15:15.300] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004190)  
In view, Mr. Chair, of the recent ambush that happened against the sitting interim chief minister,

**[03:15:27.360] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004191)  
I would also like to find out from Comelec if the initial report of the police,

**[03:15:33.320] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004192)  
those who investigate the incident, has already determined the motive behind this attempt on the life of our sitting interim chief minister.

**[03:15:48.820] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004193)  
Can we safely assume, for example, that this is a politically motivated,

**[03:15:57.360] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004194)  
act, or is it something else?

**[03:16:04.480] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004195)  
Kasi we also would like to find out if this will be an isolated case,

**[03:16:12.240] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004196)  
meaning we would like to find out if this can be avoided up to the time when BARM holds its first regular election,

**[03:16:22.580] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004197)  
kasi gusto natin orderly and peaceful.

**[03:16:24.900] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004198)  
So yun lang po ang ating gustong magkaroon.

**[03:16:27.360] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004199)  
Marinig na update sa ating napakagaling na chairman ng Commission on Elections.

**[03:16:35.040] Rep. Ziaur-Rahman "Zia" Alonto Adiong** (asr-004200)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[03:16:36.400] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004201)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Chair, Your Honors.

**[03:16:39.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004202)  
Ang eleksyon sa Bangsamoro sa September 14 ay tuloy na tuloy.

**[03:16:42.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004203)  
Walang duda at wala na dapat pang ikabahala yung mga kababayan natin.

**[03:16:47.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004204)  
Hindi na po tayo papayag ng resetting postponement.

**[03:16:49.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004205)  
At this point, walang restraining order from the Honorable Supreme Court and therefore, tuloy na tuloy po yan.

**[03:16:54.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004206)  
Walong pupo na seats ng Parliament.

**[03:16:57.360] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004207)  
May involved dyan.

**[03:16:58.940] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004208)  
Dyan po ay may 32 na parliamentary districts.

**[03:17:01.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004209)  
Meron pong korenta mula sa political parties.

**[03:17:04.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004210)  
At may walo para sa sectoral organizations.

**[03:17:06.360] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004211)  
3.393 million ang botate sa buong Bangsamoro.

**[03:17:10.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004212)  
5,212 ang kanya pong presinto.

**[03:17:15.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004213)  
At meron po siyang 1,816 na polling places o voting centers sa buong Bangsamoro.

**[03:17:22.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004214)  
Ngayon po, sa kasalukuyan,

**[03:17:25.320] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004215)  
nag-iisa lang po ang election-related incident.

**[03:17:27.360] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004216)  
First time sa kasaysayan ng Bangsamoro.

**[03:17:29.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004217)  
Of course, hindi naman po dahil sa bilang lamang.

**[03:17:32.820] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004218)  
Pero sa kasaysayan po ng Bangsamoro,

**[03:17:34.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004219)  
whether nung ARM pa at ngayon nung ito ay BARM na,

**[03:17:38.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004220)  
ay isa lang ang election-related incident.

**[03:17:41.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004221)  
In fact po, sa buong Bangsamoro, 27,

**[03:17:45.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004222)  
ang sinasabi na under red category areas,

**[03:17:48.800] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004223)  
21 po dyan ay nasa Lanao del Sur.

**[03:17:50.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004224)  
At tigitigisa ang Maguindanao del Norte, Maguindanao Sur,

**[03:17:54.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004225)  
ang Basilan, Tawi-Tawi, at Zero.

**[03:17:57.360] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004226)  
Ang SGA naman.

**[03:17:59.320] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004227)  
Yung pong 21 na red category areas,

**[03:18:02.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004228)  
maaaring sa Lanao del Sur yan,

**[03:18:04.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004229)  
pero hindi po nangangahulugan na magulo o may recent na violence

**[03:18:07.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004230)  
doon sa 21.

**[03:18:09.140] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004231)  
Kung hindi binasa lang po sa kasaysayan

**[03:18:11.240] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004232)  
nung mga lugar, dahil madami po kasing rido.

**[03:18:14.220] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004233)  
Madami po mga pangyayari ng mga nakakarang mga halalan pa before

**[03:18:17.300] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004234)  
na nagkaroon ng mga insidente,

**[03:18:18.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004235)  
pero hindi nangangahulugan na ngayon

**[03:18:20.340] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004236)  
ay may insidente ng karahasan.

**[03:18:22.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004237)  
Tama po kayo, yung isang election-related incident na yan

**[03:18:25.220] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004238)  
ay patungkol sa ating interim chief minister.

**[03:18:29.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004239)  
Lima po ang pinaghihinalaan, napinpoint na po yan kung sino,

**[03:18:32.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004240)  
at mukhang ang chief minister mismo kilala niya kung sino yan.

**[03:18:35.100] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004241)  
Sapagkat during our last command conference,

**[03:18:38.040] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004242)  
sinabi niya on record,

**[03:18:40.300] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004243)  
ibig sabihin po niya, pinapanumpaan niya,

**[03:18:42.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004244)  
walang pagganti, walang retaliation,

**[03:18:48.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004245)  
at asahan nila ngayon,

**[03:18:50.360] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004246)  
at kahit matapos ang election,

**[03:18:52.020] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004247)  
hindi gaganti siya o yung kanyang mga tauhan.

**[03:18:54.500] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004248)  
So yan po yung isang magandang statement

**[03:18:57.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004249)  
mula sa interim chief minister

**[03:19:00.300] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004250)  
sapagkat at least po kahit paano

**[03:19:02.460] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004251)  
ay naging mapayapa at naging nawala yung nabagabag yung mga kalooban

**[03:19:06.760] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004252)  
ng lahat po na naandun.

**[03:19:08.920] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004253)  
Sa kasalukuyan din po,

**[03:19:10.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004254)  
yung mga guro natin ay ongoing,

**[03:19:12.320] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004255)  
matatapos na ang training nila,

**[03:19:14.820] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004256)  
yung ating mga technical people ay nagtitraining,

**[03:19:17.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004257)  
at lahat po ng kagamitan ay nakadeploy na

**[03:19:19.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004258)  
sa lahat ng mga probinsya,

**[03:19:21.420] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004259)  
sa mga tinatawag natin na provincial hubs

**[03:19:23.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004260)  
ng komisyon.

**[03:19:24.500] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004261)  
Asahan nyo po,

**[03:19:25.760] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004262)  
sa unang linggo pa lamang na September,

**[03:19:28.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004263)  
lahat pong ito, itong linggong ito,

**[03:19:29.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004264)  
lahat pong yan ay madideliver na

**[03:19:31.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004265)  
yung mga gamit sa lahat ng munisipyo.

**[03:19:34.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004266)  
Ang mga balota po kasi ay sa treasurer's offices.

**[03:19:37.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004267)  
Yung pong mga election para pernalya,

**[03:19:38.660] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004268)  
tulad ng makina, bateriya,

**[03:19:40.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004269)  
ballot boxes,

**[03:19:41.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004270)  
yan po ay dinideliver sa mga election officer

**[03:19:44.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004271)  
ng local comelec.

**[03:19:46.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004272)  
So asahan nyo po,

**[03:19:48.080] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004273)  
meron po tayong September 12

**[03:19:49.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004274)  
na tinatawag na final testing and sealing.

**[03:19:52.380] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004275)  
Lahat po,

**[03:19:53.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004276)  
ng 5,212 na machines

**[03:19:55.840] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004277)  
sa 5,212 na presinto

**[03:19:58.420] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004278)  
ay susubukan

**[03:19:59.580] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004279)  
kung gumagana ng maayos,

**[03:20:01.520] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004280)  
magbibilang ba siya ng maayos

**[03:20:02.920] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004281)  
at makakapag-transmit ba ng maayos.

**[03:20:05.860] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004282)  
Kung sakali po may mga guro

**[03:20:07.160] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004283)  
na hindi po maghahapaglingkod

**[03:20:09.740] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004284)  
sa araw ng election,

**[03:20:11.220] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004285)  
meron po tayong more or less 2,000

**[03:20:13.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004286)  
na mga PNP personnel

**[03:20:16.300] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004287)  
na tinrain po natin

**[03:20:17.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004288)  
na pwedeng humalinhin,

**[03:20:19.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004289)  
mag-substitute o pumalit sa kanila.

**[03:20:21.480] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004290)  
Sila po ay hindi nang galing sa

**[03:20:23.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004291)  
Bangsamoro.

**[03:20:24.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004292)  
Galing po sila sa Region 10,

**[03:20:25.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004293)  
sa Region 9,

**[03:20:27.220] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004294)  
sa Region 11,

**[03:20:28.320] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004295)  
at sa Region 12.

**[03:20:29.520] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004296)  
Upang masigurado po namin

**[03:20:30.900] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004297)  
na wala pong magka-question

**[03:20:32.620] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004298)  
sa integridad at impartiality

**[03:20:34.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004299)  
ng mga yan.

**[03:20:35.920] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004300)  
Nagdagdag din po ng pwersa na

**[03:20:37.300] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004301)  
ang ating pungsan datahang lakas.

**[03:20:39.040] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004302)  
Meron po silang

**[03:20:39.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004303)  
quick reaction force

**[03:20:42.160] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004304)  
at nagdagdag din po

**[03:20:43.400] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004305)  
ang Philippine National Police

**[03:20:44.700] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004306)  
ng kanila pong pwersa.

**[03:20:46.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004307)  
So handang-handa na po kami

**[03:20:48.040] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004308)  
isagawa ang eleksyon

**[03:20:49.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004309)  
ngayon darating na September 14.

**[03:20:51.220] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004310)  
Ito po ay again,

**[03:20:52.540] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004311)  
automated election.

**[03:20:53.860] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004312)  
Sa gabi ng halalan,

**[03:20:55.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004313)  
alam na po ng lahat

**[03:20:55.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004314)  
kung sinong nanalo.

**[03:20:57.440] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004315)  
Kinabukasan po ng 15,

**[03:20:59.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004316)  
makakapagproklaman na po kami

**[03:21:01.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004317)  
ng mga kandidato.

**[03:21:02.640] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004318)  
And therefore,

**[03:21:03.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004319)  
hopefully po,

**[03:21:04.300] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004320)

**[03:21:05.420] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004321)  
na mga two days pa lang

**[03:21:06.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004322)  
after September 14.

**[03:21:09.120] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004323)  
No failure of election po,

**[03:21:10.520] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004324)  
yan po ang ating instruction

**[03:21:12.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004325)  
sa ating mga masisipag na tauhan

**[03:21:15.320] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004326)  
na nasa field sa Bangsamoro.

**[03:21:17.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004327)  
At meron din po kami

**[03:21:17.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004328)  
mga inasay na mga task force

**[03:21:19.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004329)  
mula sa main office

**[03:21:20.940] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004330)  
upang subaybayan

**[03:21:21.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004331)  
ang kandidato.

**[03:21:22.540] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004332)  
No kandak ng ating po

**[03:21:23.720] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004333)  
halalan.

**[03:21:24.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004334)  
So far po,

**[03:21:25.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004335)  
nakakatuwa,

**[03:21:26.940] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004336)

**[03:21:28.920] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004337)  
ay nagre-respond

**[03:21:31.280] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004338)  
ang ating mga kababayan

**[03:21:32.760] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004339)  
at the same time

**[03:21:34.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004340)  
yung mga command conferences natin

**[03:21:35.880] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004341)  
at ang buong commission

**[03:21:37.400] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004342)  
and bank po

**[03:21:37.960] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004343)  
ay pupunta dyan

**[03:21:38.760] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004344)  
September 11 pa lamang

**[03:21:40.400] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004345)  
hanggang sa matapos

**[03:21:41.940] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004346)  
ang proclamation

**[03:21:42.680] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004347)  
upang mas masubaybayan namin.

**[03:21:45.000] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004348)  
At may immediate action

**[03:21:46.160] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004349)

**[03:38:02.740] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004835)

**[03:38:10.740] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004839)

**[03:38:16.740] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004842)

**[03:38:22.740] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004845)

**[03:38:24.740] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004846)

**[03:38:30.740] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004849)

**[03:38:34.740] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-004851)

**[04:35:40.400] DBM Director Elena Regina S. Brillantes** (asr-005295)

**[04:42:01.440] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005387)  
Quick question lang.

**[04:42:02.440] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005388)  
What is the position of Comelec regarding the proposed

**[04:42:09.920] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005389)  
and being presented in the plenary right now,

**[04:42:14.640] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005390)  
the fixing of the term of Barangay election

**[04:42:19.000] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005391)  
and of course, the S-K election?

**[04:42:21.780] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005392)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair,

**[04:42:23.500] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005393)  
at your honors, our SDS.

**[04:42:27.260] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005394)  
As far as the Comelec is concerned,

**[04:42:28.980] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005395)  
again, we would like to reiterate,

**[04:42:30.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005396)  
it is an absolute, absolutely, absolute, absolute, absolute,

**[04:42:31.440] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005397)  
absolute authority of Congress,

**[04:42:32.200] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005398)  
the sole authority of Congress to fix the term

**[04:42:36.520] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005399)  
of Barangay and S-K officials.

**[04:42:38.760] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005400)  
Which means to say, according to the Supreme Court,

**[04:42:40.840] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005401)  
in one case, sole means to the exclusion of any other body.

**[04:42:45.060] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005402)  
And that's the reason why in 2025,

**[04:42:47.180] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005403)  
during the time that the Congress was debating

**[04:42:50.700] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005404)  
and had passed a term fixing legislation,

**[04:42:54.160] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005405)  
it was never restrained by the Supreme Court.

**[04:42:56.560] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005406)  
It was even affirmed by the honorable Supreme Court.

**[04:42:59.600] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005407)  
And so, if that is the case, go ahead.

**[04:42:59.920] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005408)  
I'd like to be the speaker.

**[04:43:00.640] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005409)  
If that is the case, then we will definitely respect the exercise of authority of Congress of that power to fix the term.

**[04:43:07.800] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005410)  
Whether it be five years, whether it be two consecutive terms, as far as the common law is concerned, we will implement whatever is the wisdom of Congress.

**[04:43:17.700] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005411)  
Were you invited in the deliberation in the Senate?

**[04:43:23.320] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005412)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[04:43:24.740] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005413)  
You gave the same position.

**[04:43:26.080] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005414)  
Yes, the same position that only Congress has the sole authority.

**[04:43:29.480] COMELEC Chairperson George Erwin M. Garcia** (asr-005415)  
In fact, Mr. Chair, the Supreme Court mentioned that when it comes to the fixing of the term, it is the sole authority of Congress.

**[04:43:39.800] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005416)  
Yes, the reason why I'm asking this is because, of course, it's important in the budget.

**[04:43:46.400] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005417)  
Remember, I asked you about postponement as against fixing of term during the deliberation of your budget, budget deliberation.

**[04:43:58.820] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005418)  
Is that right?

**[04:43:58.840] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005419)  
No, no, during the suffrage, you know, and it is quite clear the difference between fixing and postponement.

**[04:44:06.940] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005420)  
And finally, from postponement, now it's being presented in the plenary, and I think it will pass on second reading tonight while we deliberate here.

**[04:44:28.840] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005421)  
And probably it will take, maybe next week, no?

**[04:44:34.280] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005422)  
Maybe it will be approved on third reading.

**[04:44:37.060] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005423)  
Then there will be bicam, and what do you think will be the, your preparation, let's say it was approved here, it was also approved in the Senate, then of course it will be bicam.

**[04:44:50.040] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005424)  
There might be difference in the term, no?

**[04:44:53.680] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005425)  
I think the proposal of Senate is three terms, three consecutive.

**[04:45:00.000] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005426)  
terms. Right now, it's three

**[04:45:01.960] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005427)  
but however, I think

**[04:45:03.520] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005428)  
somebody will propose an

**[04:45:05.840] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005429)  
amendment for two terms.

**[04:45:08.640] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005430)  
And magbabay kami yan.

**[04:45:10.720] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005431)  
Ano yung

**[04:45:11.480] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005432)

**[04:45:15.680] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005433)

you will be asking the

Barangay Captain SK

Let's say na-approve yan

**[04:45:26.040] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005439)  
within the week in Congress.

**[04:45:27.760] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005440)  
Definitely, magkakabay

**[04:45:31.580] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005441)

**[04:45:32.680] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005442)
